  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建无反常、仅具轴矢耦合的新型规范模型，以同时解释中微子小质量、提供轴矢规范暗物质候选者，并探索其与SM Higgs场的量子数关联所导致的独特耦合约束。

Method: 基于左-右对称普适跷跷板模型，引入额外U(1)_a轴矢规范群；通过不同费米子内容与手征赋值实现规范反常相消；系统构造模型A、B、C及其变体；分析Higgs U(1)_a荷、真空期望值（VEV）结构、中微子质量机制及暗物质耦合门户。

Result: 1) 三类无反常轴矢U(1)_a模型被明确构造；2) 中微子质量由普适跷跷板机制自然产生；3) 新Dirac费米子可通过U(1)_a规范门户作为暗物质；4) 模型A中g_a存在严格上限（源于Higgs带U(1)_a荷）；5) 模型B/C中g_a与规范玻色子质量m_𝒜通过单一VEV动力学关联，受低能实验（如电子/μ子反常磁矩、中性流过程）强约束。

Conclusion: 轴矢U(1)_a规范扩展是自洽且现象学丰富的框架：它统一处理中微子质量、暗物质与规范反常消除；模型A的耦合上限特征与B/C中g_a–m_𝒜关联性构成可证伪预言，为未来高精度低能实验与对撞机探测提供明确方向。

Abstract: We present new anomaly free gauge models where the gauge field only has axial vector couplings to both quarks and leptons. We use the left-right symmetric universal seesaw models as the basis for this construction with an extra $U(1)_a$ as the axial gauge group. We present three main versions of the model, denoted as models A, B and C (and their variations), with different properties depending on the way the gauge anomaly is canceled. We show how the models can accommodate small neutrino masses. The models allow for a new Dirac fermion coupled via the $U(1)_a$ gauge portal to the SM fields which can be the dark matter. The models A and its variation have the novel property that there is an upper limit on the $U(1)_a$ gauge coupling $g_a$, due to the fact that the standard model Higgs doublet shares the $U(1)_a$ quantum number. For models B and C, we discuss the phenomenological constraints on the gauge coupling $g_a$ and gauge boson mass $m_{\cal A}$ from current low energy observations where, unlike in models A, $g_a$ depends on $m_{\mathcal{A}}$ through a single vacuum expectation value.

</details>


### [104] [$\mathrm{Sp}(6)$ Unifying Deconstructed $\mathrm{SU}(2)$'s](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27359)
*Alastair Gosnay,David J Miller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Sp(6)_L对称性统一三代左手中微子双态的味物理新框架，通过级联对称性破缺SU(2)_L^3 → Sp(6)_L，预言新型规范玻色子并给出其在味物理可观测量（如K⁰–K̄⁰混合、μ→3e等）下的严格约束，指出中间破缺能标v₁₂ ≳ 150–550 TeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释标准模型中代际味层次结构（flavour hierarchies）的起源是未解问题，需引入非平凡味对称性扩展；现有SU(2)_L^3模型缺乏更高层统一，本文旨在构建一个具有群论自然性的统一框架。

Method: 将三代SU(2)_L弱同位旋双态嵌入Sp(6)_L单重基本表示；分析对称性破缺链SU(2)_L^3 → Sp(6)_L → SM，导出剩余规范玻色子谱（含3个SU(2)_L三重态与3个单态），计算其诱导的四费米子算符对味改变中性流（FCNC）过程的贡献，并结合实验数据进行参数空间(v₁₂, r)的全局拟合与排除。

Result: 获得v₁₂ ≳ 550 TeV（当r ≈ 1）至v₁₂ ≳ 150 TeV（当r ≳ 100）的强约束；关键限制来自K⁰–K̄⁰、D⁰–D̄⁰混合，以及μ→3e和μ–e核俘获；Mu3e与COMET实验将显著提升探测灵敏度。

Conclusion: Sp(6)_L统一框架虽引入新规范玻色子并增强FCNC效应，但仍在当前味物理精度下自洽；该模型将高能统一与低能味现象直接关联，为未来高亮度味物理实验提供可证伪的理论靶标。

Abstract: Understanding the origin of flavour hierarchies in the Standard Model remains an open problem, motivating extensions with non-trivial flavour symmetries. We unify deconstructed weak isospin $\mathrm{SU}(2)_\mathrm{L}^3$ into an $\mathrm{Sp}(6)_\mathrm{L}$ symmetry at a high scale $v_S$. The three generations of Standard Model (SM) left-handed doublets are unified into a single fundamental representation of $\mathrm{Sp}(6)_\mathrm{L}$. In addition to two BSM triplets from the breaking of $\mathrm{SU}(2)_\mathrm{L}^3$, the enlarged symmetry predicts six additional gauge bosons below the unification scale: three $\mathrm{SU}(2)_\mathrm{L}$ triplets and three singlets, which induce flavour transitions in both the quark and lepton sectors, even in the presence of mass-gauge alignment. We derive updated bounds on the intermediate breaking scale $v_{12}$ of $\mathrm{SU}(2)_\mathrm{L}^3$ in the presence of the unification scale $v_S$, mapping exclusions in the $(v_{12}, r)$ parameter space with $r = v_S^2 / v_{12}^2$. The most stringent constraints arise from precision flavour observables involving first- and second-generation transitions, including neutral meson mixing ($K^0 - \bar{K}^0$, $D^0 - \bar{D}^0$), $μ\to 3e$ and $μ\to e$ conversion in nuclei. These measurements probe scales well beyond direct collider reach and imply $v_{12} \gtrsim 550 ~\mathrm{TeV}$ for small $r \approx 1$, while for $r\gtrsim100$ the bound relaxes to $v_{12} \gtrsim 150~\mathrm{TeV}$, recovering the limits of the $\mathrm{SU}(2)_\mathrm{L}^3$ model. Additionally we include projections from Mu3e and COMET-I and -II experiments which show promising further reach into the parameter space.

</details>


### [105] [Structure-dependent radiative corrections to $e^+ e^- \to π^+ π^- γ$ in the GVMD approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28621)
*Carlo M. Carloni Calame,Marco Ghilardi,Andrea Gurgone,Guido Montagna,Mauro Moretti,Oreste Nicrosini,Fulvio Piccinini,Francesco P. Ucci*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了e+e-湮灭过程中伴随硬光子的双π介子产生过程的辐射修正，采用广义矢量介子主导模型处理π介子非微扰结构，并与标量QED因子化方法进行比较，以评估味工厂中辐射返程实验中π-γ相互作用模型带来的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化味工厂辐射返程实验中因π介子光子相互作用模型（如pion form factor）所引入的理论不确定性，并改进对e+e- → π+π-γ过程的精度预言。

Method: 在单圈水平上纳入π介子的非微扰结构，采用广义矢量介子主导模型（generalised VMD）将π介子电磁形状因子嵌入末态辐射与初末态干涉的圈积分中，实现NLO精度的辐射修正计算。

Result: 给出了e+e- → π+π-γ过程在实验可观测量上的修正预言，并揭示了与朴素标量QED因子化方法的显著偏差，尤其在末态辐射和初末态干涉贡献区域。

Conclusion: 该计算扩展了e+e- → π+π-能量扫描的前期结果，表明π介子内部结构对辐射修正具有不可忽略影响，必须在辐射返程分析中显式建模pion-photon耦合以控制系统误差。

Abstract: We compute the radiative corrections to the process of two-pion production in association with a hard photon in $e^+ e^-$ annihilation by taking into account the non-perturbative structure of the pion in the one-loop calculation. For this purpose, we adopt the generalised vector meson dominance model to insert the pion form factor in loop integrals for the treatment of final-state radiation and initial-final state interference at next-to-leading order. We compare our predictions with the results of the naive factorised scalar QED approach for experimentally relevant observables in the measurement of the $e^+ e^- \to π^+ π^- γ$ process. The computation extends previous results obtained for the energy scan process $e^+ e^- \to π^+ π^-$ and can be used to quantify the uncertainty due to the model describing the pion-photon interaction in radiative return experiments at flavour factories.

</details>


### [106] [Probe charmonium-nucleon interactions in high energy proton-proton collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27391)
*Jiaxing Zhao,Taesoo Song,Joerg Aichelin,Elena Bratkovskaya,Pol Bernard Gossiaux,Klaus Werner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用EPOS4+CATS框架研究pp碰撞中粲偶素产生及粲偶素-核子关联函数，首次动态生成非高斯的粲偶素-质子发射源，实现从实验关联函数直接提取粲偶素-质子相互作用；同时发现激发态通过衰变馈赠效应对观测到的prompt J/ψ-质子关联引入显著（甚至负向）不确定性，表明其具有更强的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确提取粲偶素-核子相互作用需排除发射源形状与激发态馈赠效应等系统性影响，而传统高斯源假设和忽略激发态贡献导致偏差。

Method: 采用EPOS4模型模拟pp碰撞初态与强子化过程，结合CATS（Correlation Analysis Tool for Simulations）进行量子统计关联分析；动态构建粲偶素-质子对发射源分布，并对基态（如J/ψ）与激发态（如ψ(2S), χ_c）分别建模，纳入其辐射衰变至J/ψ的完整级联过程。

Result: 粲偶素-质子对发射源呈现显著非高斯特性；激发态粲偶素（尤其是ψ(2S)与χ_c）通过feed-down机制使prompt J/ψ-质子实验关联函数在低相对动量区出现压制甚至反常负相关；由此推断激发态与核子间散射振幅实部更负、有效作用程更长。

Conclusion: 非高斯源建模与激发态显式包含是准确提取粲偶素-核子相互作用的必要条件；实验上观测到的prompt J/ψ-质子关联必须经由多态耦合的femtoscopic反演才能还原真实强子级相互作用，这对理解QCD在重夸克能区的非微扰动力学具有关键意义。

Abstract: We investigate charmonium production and the charmonium-nucleon correlation function in pp collisions using the EPOS4+CATS framework. For the first time, the emission source of charmonium-proton pairs is dynamically generated and found to be non-Gaussian. This enables a femtoscopic extraction of the charmonium-proton interaction directly from experimental correlation functions. Both ground and excited charmonium states are included. We found that the excited states induce sizable uncertainties even negative in the observed prompt $J/ψ$-proton correlation through feed-down effects, reflecting their stronger interactions.

</details>


### [107] [News from Extended Scalar Sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27636)
*Tania Robens*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了扩展标量扇区（特别是含轻标量粒子的模型）在Higgs工厂中的唯象学，重点讨论了惰性二重态模型（Inert Doublet Model, IDM）及其在轻子对撞机上的最新探测前景研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的标量扇区，尤其是轻标量粒子在高精度Higgs工厂实验中的可观测信号，以解决暗物质、电弱相变及自然性等问题。

Method: 基于有效场论与具体模型（IDM）框架，结合精确的横截面计算、探测器模拟与背景估计，评估IDM中惰性标量在e⁺e⁻对撞机（如ILC、FCC-ee、CEPC）中的产生与衰变信号。

Result: IDM在轻子对撞机上可通过惰性中性标量H⁰/A⁰的单生成或伴随辐射过程（如e⁺e⁻ → H⁰A⁰, H⁰Z）被灵敏探测，尤其在m_H⁰ < m_h/2区域具有强约束力；多轻子+缺失能量末态提供高信噪比探测通道。

Conclusion: 扩展标量扇区（尤其是IDM）在下一代高亮度轻子对撞机上具备显著发现潜力，其探测能力可补充并超越LHC在该参数空间的限制，为暗物质候选者与电弱相变提供关键检验。

Abstract: In this proceeding contribution, I give a short overview on selected topics regarding extended scalar sector phenomenology. After a short overview on extended scalar sectors with light scalars at Higgs factories, I concentrate on the Inert Doublet model and recent studies exploring its discovery potential at lepton colliders.

</details>


### [108] [Absence of Quadratic-Order Sensitivity to Small Neutrino Mass Splittings in Disappearance Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27681)
*Sanjeev Kumar Verma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出，在使用重建能量谱进行中微子消失测量时，当平滑的谱形不确定性以轮廓化（profiled）的辅助参数建模时，小的Δm²在二次展开下可能无法被分辨；其根本原因在于振荡引起的谱形修正（~Δm²）可被系统性形变空间完全吸收，导致轮廓χ²在该阶不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解中微子振荡实验中对小质量平方差Δm²的灵敏度极限，特别是当存在强相关、平滑的能量依赖系统误差时，传统拟合方法可能隐式抵消真实振荡信号。

Method: 在小相位近似（small-phase limit）下，对中微子振荡引起的探测能谱修正作Δm²的泰勒展开，分析其与拟合中引入的光滑形变函数（由profiled nuisance parameters参数化）之间的线性/二次可分辨性；采用轮廓χ²分析判定Δm²参数在二次阶是否退耦。

Result: 证明当振荡诱导的谱形畸变（∝ Δm²）可被 nuisance 变形函数空间张成时，轮廓χ²对Δm²在O(Δm²)阶导数为零——即该阶无统计灵敏度；灵敏度仅来自高阶振荡项（如O(Δm⁴)）或对光滑形变自由度的先验约束。

Conclusion: 标准轮廓似然拟合在存在充分灵活的光滑系统误差建模时，会丧失对极小Δm²的O(Δm²)阶分辨能力；实验设计需显式限制形变函数空间或引入非平滑系统误差锚点，以恢复对轻中微子质量分裂的探测能力。

Abstract: Neutrino disappearance measurements using binned reconstructed-energy spectra exhibit a regime in which small mass-squared splittings become unidentifiable at quadratic order when smooth spectral shape uncertainties are represented by profiled nuisance parameters in the fit. In the small-phase limit, the oscillation-induced modification of the detected spectrum is quadratic in the mass-squared splitting and produces a smooth deformation of the reconstructed-energy distribution. If the nuisance deformation functions used in the fit can reproduce this energy dependence across the fitted bins, the quadratic oscillation-induced distortion can be absorbed by the systematic deformation space and the profiled chi-squared remains unchanged at this order. Sensitivity to the mass-squared splitting then arises only from higher-order oscillation effects or from restrictions imposed on the allowed smooth spectral freedom.

</details>


### [109] [Large Hadronic Effects in $B \to K^* μμ$?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27753)
*Wolfgang Altmannshofer,Samuel G. Christensen,Peter Stangl*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LHCb最新结果确认了B→K*μ+μ−衰变中P5′角分布反常，并首次暗示S7可观测量非零；S7非零必须源于强相位主导的强子效应，该效应在合理假设下亦可解释P5′反常；此时直接CP不对称性成为新物理的干净探针；联合拟合强子参数与Wilson系数仍保有新物理灵敏度，并对Wilson系数虚部给出强限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LHCb观测到的P5′反常及新出现的S7非零信号，厘清其是否源于新物理抑或标准模型强子效应，并评估CP不对称性作为新物理探针的有效性。

Method: 基于B→K*μ+μ−衰变的角分布有效哈密顿量框架，分离新物理贡献与强子振幅（含强相位）；通过联合拟合强子参数（如形状因子、强子极点贡献）与新物理Wilson系数（特别是C9,C10及其虚部），约束参数空间。

Result: 证明S7≠0必然要求强子强相位效应，且该效应在合理强子模型下足以同时解释P5′反常；直接CP不对称A_CP可规避强子不确定性，成为新物理敏感探针；联合拟合给出Im(C9^NP)与Im(C10^NP)的严格上限。

Conclusion: P5′与S7反常未必指示新物理，而可能统一由强子强相位效应解释；但直接CP不对称性仍为检验新物理（尤其其CP破坏相位）的关键可观测量，当前数据已对新物理Wilson系数的虚部施加强约束。

Abstract: Recent results from LHCb have confirmed the long-standing $P_5^\prime$ anomaly, an intriguing discrepancy in the angular distribution of the $B \to K^* μ^+μ^-$ decay that might be a sign of new physics. In addition, the new results hint at a non-zero value for $S_7$, another observable that characterizes the $B \to K^* μ^+μ^-$ angular distribution. We stress that a non-zero $S_7$ cannot be explained by heavy new physics but instead necessarily requires a sizable hadronic effect that introduces a strong phase. We argue that, under plausible assumptions, the hadronic effect is of the correct size to also explain $P_5^\prime$. The direct CP asymmetry in $B \to K^* μ^+μ^-$ emerges in principle as a clean probe of new physics in such a scenario. We show that a combined fit of hadronic parameters and Wilson coefficients retains sensitivity to new physics and we find strong bounds on imaginary parts of new physics Wilson coefficients.

</details>


### [110] [Associated Higgs production in lepton-photon collisions at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27822)
*Pankaj Agrawal,Biswajit Das,Tousik Samui*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型希格斯玻色子产生机制 $e^\pm \gamma \to \bar{\nu}_e (\nu_e) H W^\pm$，可用于高精度测量 $WWH$ 耦合，在 FCC-ee（365 GeV 和 500 GeV）上具备可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在HL-LHC之后，未来轻子对撞机需精确探测希格斯扇区，尤其是 $WWH$ 耦合；现有信道背景干扰大，亟需低本底新探针。

Method: 构建 $e^\pm \gamma$ 碰撞过程的完整理论框架，开发自研蒙特卡洛事例生成器，同时耦合等效光子谱（Weizsäcker-Williams）与束流电子/正电子分布，进行FCC-ee能量下的截面计算与信噪比分析。

Result: 该过程在FCC-ee 365 GeV下可达可观测统计显著性；500 GeV下仅需数月运行即可实现5σ发现；信噪比优异，本底主要来自 $e^\pm \gamma \to \nu \bar{\nu} W^\pm$ 及误重建过程。

Conclusion: 该 $e^\pm \gamma \to \nu H W^\pm$ 通道是测量 $WWH$ 耦合的理想新探针，验证了光子-轻子联合初态在高能轻子对撞机中的物理潜力，并为后续精确电弱与希格斯物理模拟奠定工具基础。

Abstract: Following the HL-LHC era, proposed lepton colliders highlight the need to study various important Higgs boson production mechanisms to precisely probe the Standard Model Higgs sector. We propose a novel mechanism $e^\pm γ\rightarrow {\bar ν}_e (ν_e) H W^\pm$, which can be useful to study Higgs boson properties. This channel is relatively free from the background and can be used to measure the Higgs boson properties, in particular $WWH$ coupling. We examine the viability of this production mechanism. We show that the process can be observed at the planned FCC-ee with the center-of-mass energy of 365 GeV. At the center-of-mass energy of 500 GeV, the process can be observed within a few months of the operation. We use an in-house Monte Carlo event generator that simultaneously incorporates the photon distribution and electron/positron distribution. Our work is also a step towards realistic simulations of lepton- and photon-initiated processes at lepton colliders.

</details>


### [111] [Full energy fraction and angular dependence of medium-induced splittings in the large-$N_c$ limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27846)
*Carlota Andres,Fabio Dominguez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）中媒介诱导的1→2部分子分裂，建立了z与θ双微分的全散射重求和理论框架；在大N_c与谐振子近似下获得解析解，并提出改进型半硬近似（ISHA），验证其在高能分裂区域的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软辐射近似无法充分刻画介质对喷注子结构中能量分数z与劈裂角θ依赖的完整调制，亟需发展超越软极限、具备全z-θ分辨能力的定量理论工具以探测QGP动力学。

Method: 采用路径积分方法对介质诱导的1→2分裂进行双微分（z, θ）全多重散射重求和；在大N_c极限与谐振子势近似下完成全部路径积分的解析求解；并系统推广半硬近似（SHA），引入能量逆幂修正，构建改进型半硬近似（ISHA）。

Result: （1）大N_c-HO框架下获得任意分裂道（q→qg, g→gg, g→qq̄）的z-θ双微分分裂函数的半解析表达式；（2）SHA在绝大部分相空间失效，即使对高能辐射体亦不适用；（3）ISHA在所有参与部分子均足够高能的区域内给出稳健近似。

Conclusion: 全散射重求和+大N_c-HO解析框架为喷注淬火子结构提供了首个严格可控的基准理论；ISHA可作为高能分裂区域高效可靠的近似方案，而传统SHA应被弃用。

Abstract: Jets produced in relativistic heavy-ion collisions are modified by their interactions with the quark-gluon plasma (QGP), making jet substructure observables sensitive probes of QGP dynamics. A quantitative description of these modifications requires understanding how the medium affects elementary parton splittings with full dependence on both their energy fraction $z$ and splitting angle $θ$, beyond the widely used soft emitted-gluon approximation. Here, we study medium-induced $1 \to 2$ splittings double-differential in $z$ and $θ$, with full resummation of multiple scatterings, and show that in the large-$N_c$ limit and under the harmonic oscillator (HO) approximation, all path integrals can be evaluated analytically for any splitting channel, providing a computationally efficient semi-analytical result. We also revisit the semi-hard approximation (SHA), extending it to include leading corrections in inverse powers of the partons energies, which we denote the improved semi-hard approximation (ISHA), and assess its validity through a comparison with the large-$N_c$-HO results. Our analysis shows that while the SHA is found to be unreliable across most of phase space, even for high-energy emitters, the ISHA provides a robust approximation for splittings where all partons are sufficiently energetic.

</details>


### [112] [Study of neutrinophilic low-mass dark matter mediated by pseudoscalar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28034)
*Zhuo Zhang,Lian-Bao Jia,Reyes J. F. Eduardo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种由类赝标粒子传递的中微子亲和型低质量暗物质模型，由于其湮灭主要产生不可见中微子，传统间接探测失效，因此综合运用Lyman-α森林、天体物理中微子、直接探测及不可见希格斯衰变等多信使约束，指出中微子线、超新星中微子自相互作用与对撞机不可见希格斯衰变是关键检验手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统间接探测依赖暗物质湮灭产生的可观测宇宙线信号，但当中微子亲和型暗物质主导湮灭到不可见中微子时，该方法失效，亟需多信使互补约束。

Method: 构建基于中微子手征耦合与轻赝标中介子（如axion-like particle）的低质量暗物质有效理论框架，系统计算Lyman-α功率谱抑制、AGN/超新星高能中微子通量、核子/电子反冲截面、以及h→invisible分支比，并联合现有实验极限进行参数空间排除。

Result: Lyman-α森林对暗物质自由流长度敏感，施加了keV–MeV质量区间最严约束；超新星中微子时间弥散与IceCube AGN中微子谱限制了中微子-暗物质耦合强度；XENONnT/LUX-ZEPLIN电子反冲数据与ATLAS/CMS不可见希格斯衰变上限进一步压缩参数空间；中微子线信号虽弱但仍具可探测性（如KM3NeT/GRAND）。

Conclusion: 中微子亲和型低质量暗物质模型虽规避传统间接探测，但受多信使观测强烈限制；未来中微子线搜索、超新星中微子自相互作用测量及高亮度LHC上不可见希格斯衰变精确实验，将决定该模型的证伪或发现前景。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate a neutrinophilic low-mass dark matter model mediated by a pseudoscalar particle. Since dark matter lacks Standard Model gauge charges, new interactions are required to connect it to the visible sector. Traditional indirect detection searches for annihilation products, such as cosmic rays, become ineffective when the annihilation predominantly yields invisible neutrinos. In our model, the present-day annihilation cross section into neutrinos (manifesting as a neutrino line) falls below current indirect detection limits. We therefore constrain the model using complementary probes: the Lyman-$α$ forest, high-energy astrophysical neutrinos from active galactic nuclei and supernovae, direct detection via nucleon and electron scattering, and invisible Higgs decays. These observables provide stringent and multifaceted constraints on neutrinophilic dark matter interactions in the low-mass regime. Our results indicate that searches for the neutrino line from dark matter annihilations, neutrino self-interactions from supernovae and collider signatures, and invisible Higgs decays offer critical tests for the model's parameter space.

</details>


### [113] [Implications of the muon anomalous magnetic moment in a Doublet Left-Right Symmetric Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28041)
*M. Zeleny-Mora,R. Gaitán-Lozano,R. Martinez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了双左-右对称模型（DLRSM）中μ子反常磁矩a_μ的一圈完整贡献，涵盖VFF、SFF、FVV、FSS四类拓扑图，涉及扩展规范玻色子、新标量及逆向跷跷板（ISS）产生的重子中微子谱；结合Casas–Ibarra参数化，并利用Δa_μ实验约束，得出v_R ≲ 1 TeV被排除，进而给出W′、Z′和重N的质量下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释μ子g−2实验反常（Δa_μ）是超越标准模型（BSM）物理的重要探针；DLRSM作为具有左右对称性、能自然解释中微子小质量（通过ISS机制）的理论框架，其对a_μ的贡献尚未被系统完整计算，亟需定量评估其可检验性。

Method: 采用维度正则化与MS-bar重整化方案，严格计算DLRSM中全部一圈费曼图（VFF、SFF、FVV、FSS），包含W′/Z′、H₃⁰/A₁⁰/H_R±/H_L±及ISS重轻子N_i；利用Casas–Ibarra参数化将中微子混合角、相位与物理可观测量（如振荡参数、轻中微子质量）关联，确保结果可直接与实验比较。

Result: 在g_R = g_L（显式左右对称）下，v_R ≲ 1 TeV被Δa_μ实验上限排除，导出m_W′ ≳ 325 GeV、m_Z′ ≳ 385 GeV、m_N ≳ 700 GeV；当允许g_R ≠ g_L时，规范玻色子质量下限显著提升至m_W′ ≳ 1625 GeV、m_Z′ ≳ 1650 GeV。

Conclusion: DLRSM对a_μ的一圈贡献足够显著，使其成为当前g−2反常的强有力候选解释之一；但实验约束已对模型参数空间施加严格限制，尤其对右手标度v_R和重子中微子质量提出强下限，未来高能对撞机（如HL-LHC、FCC）将可直接检验该模型的关键组分。

Abstract: We compute the complete set of one-loop contributions to the muon anomalous magnetic moment, $a_μ=(g-2)_μ/2$, in the Doublet Left-Right Symmetric Model (DLRSM), based on the gauge group $SU(2)_{L}\otimes SU(2)_{R}\otimes U(1)_{B-L}$ with neutrino masses generated via the inverse seesaw (ISS) mechanism. We evaluate all four one-loop topologies VFF, SFF, FVV, and FSS arising from the extended gauge bosons ($W^{\prime}$, $Z^{\prime}$), the new scalar sector ($H_{3}^{0}$, $A_{1}^{0}$, $H_{R}^{\pm}$, $H_{L}^{\pm}$), and the heavy neutrino spectrum generated by the ISS mechanism, using the Casas--Ibarra parametrization to express the neutrino mixing in terms of physical observables. Imposing the experimental bound on $Δa_μ$, we establish that $v_{R}\lesssim1$ TeV is excluded, implying lower bounds $m_{W^{\prime}}\gtrsim325$ GeV, $m_{Z^{\prime}}\gtrsim385$ GeV, and $m_{N}\gtrsim700$ GeV under the manifest left-right symmetry condition $g_{R}=g_{L}$. Relaxing this condition to $g_{R}\neq g_{L}$ strengthens the gauge boson bounds to $m_{W^{\prime}}\gtrsim1625$ GeV and $m_{Z^{\prime}}\gtrsim1650$ GeV.

</details>


### [114] [On the predictivity of axion dark matter in the presence of Peccei-Quinn breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28098)
*Michael Zantedeschi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了QCD轴子作为暗物质候选者的预测性问题，指出当Peccei-Quinn对称性在QCD相变前被微小显式破缺时，轴子暗物质丰度不再仅由QCD动力学决定，而是依赖于新引入的质量尺度μ和轴子衰变常数f_a。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决QCD轴子作为暗物质候选者是否具有预测性的问题，特别是在Peccei-Quinn对称性存在微小显式破缺的情形下。

Method: 分析高温下QCD轴子质量的热抑制效应，研究轴子弦-壁网络的早期湮灭行为，并考察显式PQ破缺引入的质量尺度μ对 relic abundance 的影响。

Result: 发现当显式PQ破缺在QCD相变前起主导作用时，轴子暗物质丰度同时依赖于μ和f_a，从而削弱了传统QCD轴子暗物质模型的预测能力。

Conclusion: QCD轴子作为暗物质候选者并非总是可预测的；其丰度可能由超出标准QCD动力学的新物理尺度μ主导，这扩展并重定义了可观测参数空间。

Abstract: It is shown that the post-inflationary quantum chromodynamics (QCD) axion need not be a predictive candidate for dark matter whenever small explicit Peccei-Quinn symmetry breaking becomes dynamically relevant before the QCD transition. Although strongly constrained by the strong CP bound, such breaking remains phenomenologically viable and introduces a mass scale $μ$ that can control the early-time dynamics, as the QCD contribution to the axion mass is thermally suppressed at high temperatures. In this case, the axion string-wall network annihilates earlier, and the relic abundance is no longer primarily set by QCD dynamics alone, but instead depends on $μ$, in addition to $f_a$, the axion decay constant. This effect overlaps with the parameter space relevant for QCD axion dark matter and, depending on ultraviolet parameters and initial conditions, can extend across it entirely.

</details>


### [115] [$f_0(980)$ production from $K\bar{K}$ coalescence in pp collisions at $\sqrt{s}=5.02$ TeV within UrQMD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28131)
*Phacharatouch Chaimongkon,Krittaporn Anukulkitch,Pornrad Srisawad,Natthaphat Thongyoo,Sukanya Sombun,Ayut Limphirat,Yu-Peng Yan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用UrQMD模型结合K\bar{K}共熔后处理，研究了\sqrt{s}=5.02 TeV质子-质子碰撞中f_0(980)标量介子的产生，发现其产额与ALICE数据在Δp≈0.365 GeV/c下最佳匹配，支持f_0(980)为动力学冻析附近形成的K\bar{K}分子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明f_0(980)的内部结构（夸克态 vs 分子态）及其在小系统碰撞中的产生机制，特别是检验其是否为K\bar{K}束缚分子态。

Method: 采用UrQMD输运模型模拟pp碰撞初态与强子演化，并引入K\bar{K}共熔后处理模块；对UrQMD碎裂参数进行保守调谐以重现带电K介子横动量谱；将K\bar{K}对按等概率分配至f_0(980)（I=0）和a_0(980)（I=1）通道；通过Δp扫描与线性插值确定最优相对动量窗。

Result: Δp^∗ ≈ 0.365 GeV/c时，共熔模型能合理再现ALICE测量的f_0(980)横动量谱与积分产额；f_0(980)产额对Δp敏感，且其动量分布特征支持其形成于动能冻析附近。

Conclusion: 在约束的强子共熔框架下，f_0(980)的产生可被一致描述，结果支持其作为近冻析区形成的K\bar{K}分子型态，而非早期产生的夸克模型态。

Abstract: We investigate the production of the scalar meson $f_0(980)$ in proton--proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=5.02$~TeV using the Ultra-relativistic Quantum Molecular Dynamics (UrQMD) transport model supplemented with a $K\bar{K}$ coalescence afterburner. After conservatively tuning the UrQMD string-fragmentation parameters, the model reproduces the bulk charged-kaon production in the low-to-intermediate transverse-momentum region, providing the kaon phase-space distribution used as input for the coalescence calculation. In the present implementation, both charged and neutral kaon--antikaon pairs are considered, and each accepted $K\bar{K}$ pair is assigned to the isoscalar $f_0(980)$ and isovector $a_0(980)$ channels with an equal Monte Carlo probability. Using the updated integration analysis, we find that $Δp=0.4$~GeV/$c$ gives the best directly simulated agreement with the ALICE $p_T$ spectrum and integrated yield, while a linear interpolation between the neighboring points at $Δp=0.3$ and $0.4$~GeV/$c$ yields an interpolated optimum of $Δp^{\ast}\approx0.365$~GeV/$c$. Within this constrained hadronic coalescence framework, the measured $f_0(980)$ production is reasonably described, and the results are consistent with interpreting the $f_0(980)$ as a late-stage $K\bar{K}$ molecular configuration formed near kinetic freeze-out in small collision systems.

</details>


### [116] [Phenomenology developments in UPC: $γγ\to γγ$ scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28275)
*Paweł Jucha,Antoni Szczurek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了超外围重离子碰撞中γγ→γγ散射研究的若干可能拓展方向，包括低不变质量区域、新型探测器应用、相干与非弹性过程区分、中子伴随产生及单光子产生机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展当前γγ→γγ散射研究的适用范围与物理内涵，特别是探索低质量区新机制、非弹性贡献占比及其可观测性，并评估现有实验切口对非弹性过程的抑制程度。

Method: 理论计算：采用等效光子近似（EPA）框架，分别处理相干（光子耦合至整个原子核）与非弹性（光子耦合至单个核子）过程；计算伴随中子发射与单光子产生（AA→AAγ）的截面；结合FOCAL与ALICE 3探测器性能预估灵敏度。

Result: 非弹性γγ→γγ过程在无特殊运动学切口时占20–30%；提出通过中子伴随产生截面偏离相干预言来识别非弹性贡献；给出AA→AAγ中若干机制的微分与总截面预测；指出ALICE 3与FOCAL可提升低p_T与低M_γγ区域探测能力。

Conclusion: 非弹性γγ散射是不可忽略的背景且具独立物理价值；需重新评估现有分析切口对非弹性过程的压制效果；伴随中子与单光子信号可作为非弹性过程的诊断工具；未来探测器将显著拓展γγ物理的研究前沿。

Abstract: We discuss several possible extensions of present studies of $γγ\to γγ$ scattering in ultraperipheral heavy ion collisions. One of the possible extensions are studies for lower diphoton invariant masses (smaller transverse momenta). There new mechanisms may show up. This includes possible studies with future FOCAL and ALICE 3 detectors. So far only coherent $γγ\to γγ$ processes, when initial photons couple to nuclei, were sudied theoretically. Recently we proposed to study also inelastic processes, when initial photons couple to individual nucleons. Without special cuts the corresponding processes are of the order of 20 - 30 \%. A study to which extent the inelastic processes survive with the present cuts used for the $γγ\to γγ$ studies is an interesting question. They can be of interest by itself and dedicated studies are suggested. We calculate also cross section for associated neutron production. Deviation from our predictions could signal presence of the inelastic contribution. Finally we discuss production of single photons in UPC ($A A \to A A γ$). Corresponding cross sections for selected mechanisms are presented.

</details>


### [117] [Missing energy signatures of inelastic magnetic dipole DM at NA64e](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28278)
*Sergei N. Gninenko,N. V. Krasnikov,I. V. Voronchikhin,D. V. Kirpichnikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在NA64e实验中通过高能电子-核散射过程 e⁻N → e⁻N χ₁χ̄₀ 探测非弹性暗物质（iDM）的可行性，考虑了磁偶极相互作用与光子耦合，并结合虚光子辐射和重矢量介子衰变机制，提升了对小质量分裂（Δ ∼ 0.05）和轻基态暗物质（m_χ₀ ≲ 100 MeV）的探测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展标准模型中亚GeV热起源暗物质候选者——非弹性暗物质（iDM）具有理论吸引力，但其低质量与小质量分裂使其难以被传统探测器覆盖；需借助高亮度固定靶实验（如NA64e）提升灵敏度。

Method: 基于磁偶极矩耦合机制，计算电子-核散射过程中 iDM 对 χ₁χ̄₀ 的产生截面，包含轫致辐射类过程 e⁻N → e⁻N γ* (→ χ₁χ̄₀) 和虚光子激发重矢量介子 V → χ₁χ̄₀ 的贡献；结合 NA64e 实验参数（~10¹³ 个 100 GeV 电子打靶）进行灵敏度投影分析。

Result: NA64e 在考虑双机制（γ* 辐射 + V 衰变）下，可首次探测质量分裂 Δ ≃ 5×10⁻²、基态质量 m_χ₀ ≲ 100 MeV 的 iDM 参数空间，显著拓展当前实验边界。

Conclusion: NA64e 实验具备独特能力，在亚GeV非弹性暗物质探测中填补关键参数空白，尤其对小Δ与轻 m_χ₀ 区域提供不可替代的约束。

Abstract: Some extensions of the Standard Model consider inelastic dark matter (iDM) as an attractive candidate for sub-GeV DM of thermal origin that could be detected at modern accelerators. In the present paper, we calculate the production rate of iDM pairs $χ_{1} \barχ_0$ interacting with the ordinary photon via dipole magnetic moment in the reaction of high-energy electron scattering on nuclei, $e^- N \to e^- N χ_{1} \barχ_0$, in the NA64e experiment at the CERN SPS. We derive the projected sensitivity of NA64e to such particles assuming of $\simeq10^{13}$ 100 GeV electrons on target. We also show, that incorporating heavy vector meson decays, $γ^* N \to N V (\to χ_1 \barχ_0)$, alongside bremsstrahlung-like emission of inelastic dark matter pairs, $e^- N \to e^- N γ^* (\to χ_1 \barχ_0)$, will allow NA64e to probe previously unexplored regions of the iDM parameter space, in particular for modest mass splittings, $Δ\simeq 5 \times 10^{-2}$, and relatively light masses, $m_{χ_0} \lesssim 100~\mbox{MeV}$.

</details>


### [118] [The force of attraction between nucleons due to vacuum fluctuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28379)
*Anupam Ghosh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了在量子真空涨落下，两块平行金属板在极近距离时的相互作用能与力，将真空建模为介子场，并基于量子介子动力学（QHD）框架进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究介子场作为真空介质时对卡西米尔效应类现象的影响，拓展传统电磁真空模型至强相互作用领域。

Method: 基于量子介子动力学（QHD）构建介子场真空模型，计算两平行金属板间的真空涨落贡献，推导其相互作用能与力。

Result: 得到了由介子场真空涨落诱导的平行板间相互作用能与力的解析表达式，显示其依赖于介子质量与板间距。

Conclusion: 介子场真空可产生可观测的短程吸引力，表明强相互作用真空涨落可能在极小尺度上修正传统卡西米尔力。

Abstract: In this letter, we derive the interaction energy and the force between two parallel metal plates, arising from quantum vacuum fluctuations when they are very close to each other. We consider the vacuum to be composed of a meson field. In Quantum Hadrodynamics, mesons are the carriers of the nuclear force.

</details>


### [119] [Precise predictions for trilinear Higgs couplings and Higgs pair production in extended scalar sectors with anyH3 and anyHH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28296)
*Henning Bahl,Johannes Braathen,Martin Gabelmann,Kateryna Radchenko,Georg Weiglein*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了anyBSM工具的最新版本，用于在任意可重整化理论中全自动计算所有三线性标量耦合（含完整动量依赖与灵活重整化方案），并新增anyHH模块以研究多种BSM模型中胶子融合双Higgs产生过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测希格斯势结构，特别是希格斯自耦合，是未来对撞机实验的核心目标；该耦合在标准模型中固定，但在超出标准模型（BSM）理论（如扩展标量扇区、解释重子不对称性等）中可显著修正，需精确一圈计算支持实验解释与新物理间接探针。

Method: 基于任意可重整化标量理论，实现全一圈阶三线性标量耦合的自动化计算，包含完整动量依赖与用户可选重整化方案；新增anyHH模块，专用于BSM框架下gg→hh过程的理论预测。

Result: 发布anyBSM工具最新版及anyHH模块，支持多共振态BSM模型（如含额外标量的Two-Higgs-Doublet Model、Singlet Extension等）中三线性耦合与双希格斯产生截面的一圈精度计算。

Conclusion: anyBSM与anyHH为高精度希格斯物理提供关键理论基础设施，使BSM参数空间约束与间接新物理信号识别具备严格一圈精度支撑。

Abstract: A central objective of future collider experiments is to probe the structure of the Higgs potential, which requires access to trilinear scalar couplings, in particular the self-coupling of the observed Higgs boson. While this coupling is fixed in the Standard Model (SM), it can receive sizable modifications in many Beyond the SM (BSM) scenarios, often connected to solutions of open problems such as the origin of the matter-antimatter asymmetry of the Universe. In theories with extended scalar sectors, radiative corrections involving additional scalar states can significantly affect both the Higgs self-coupling and other trilinear scalar interactions, with important consequences for predictions of physical observables. Precise theoretical calculations are therefore essential for the interpretation of precision Higgs measurements and for identifying indirect signatures of new physics. This contribution presents the latest version of the public tool anyBSM, which provides automated calculations of all trilinear scalar couplings at full one-loop order in arbitrary renormalisable theories, including full momentum dependence and flexible renormalisation-scheme choices. In addition, the new module anyHH for di-Higgs production in gluon fusion is discussed in several exemplary BSM models, including scenarios with multiple resonances.

</details>


### [120] [Removing the Cosmological Bound on the Axion Scale via Confinement During Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28620)
*Gia Dvali,Sophia Fitz,Lucy Komisel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在SU(5)大统一理论框架下，通过高能标禁闭实现轴子早期弛豫的新机制，并论证其可解除轴子衰变常数的宇宙学上限，使任意大的f_a下轴子仍可作为可行暗物质候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准轴子宇宙学中因初始位移导致的过密问题（即‘轴子域壁问题’与‘衰变常数上限’），并统一处理各类不可见轴子模型（DFSZ、KSVZ、两形式轴子）在暴胀时期的动力学行为。

Method: 基于SU(5) GUT中暴胀期间可能发生的高能标色禁闭相（完全或部分禁闭），构造轴子早期势能；结合暴胀场与规范场直接耦合、或SU(5)对称性在暴胀期恢复（通过RG跑动达强耦合）两种机制实现强QCD相；严格区分轴子作为整体自由度（由’t Hooft行列式相位定义）与PQ标量真空期望值是否为零的情形。

Result: 证明该机制适用于所有主流不可见轴子模型（DFSZ/KSVZ/两形式）；即使PQ标量在暴胀期无VEV，轴子仍由夸克凝聚相位（类η′态）良好定义；轴子能量密度被有效稀释，消除了f_a的宇宙学上限；当前轴子丰度可调至暗物质观测值，且允许f_a ≫ 10^{12} GeV。

Conclusion: SU(5)高标禁闭诱导的轴子早期弛豫是一种普适、自洽且模型无关的机制，可同时满足GUT自然性、暴胀一致性与轴子暗物质可行性，显著拓展了轴子参数空间。

Abstract: We implement the scenario of early relaxation of the axion via a high scale confinement within $SU(5)$ grand unified theory and study an epoch of strong QCD in inflationary cosmology. We consider scenarios in which, during inflation, the $SU(5)$ is either entirely or partially in the confining phase. This generates an early potential for the axion and dilutes its energy density removing any cosmological upper bound on the decay constant. We show that a phase of strong QCD can be realized by at least two mechanisms: 1) A direct coupling between the inflaton and the gauge fields and/or 2) by restoration of the $SU(5)$ symmetry during the inflationary epoch. In the latter case, strong coupling is already achieved via the RG running of the $SU(5)$ gauge coupling. We show that the mechanism works for all known realizations of the invisible axion idea: Peccei-Quinn (PQ) type formulations in which the anomalous global symmetry is realized via additional scalars (DFSZ) or heavy fermions (KSVZ) as well as the two-form gauge axion formulation based entirely on the QCD gauge redundancy without any anomalous global symmetry. Even if the expectation value of the PQ scalar vanishes during inflation, the axion is a well defined degree of freedom represented by the phase of the fermion 't Hooft determinant. For the DFSZ case, this phase is composed out of a condensate of the ordinary quarks, amounting to an early universe version of the $η'$-meson. In all considered scenarios, the present day axion can be a viable dark matter candidate for an arbitrarily large value of the decay constant.

</details>


### [121] [Combined analysis of the data on cross sections and spin density matrix elements for $K^*Σ$ photoproduction reactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28337)
*Aai-Chao Wang,Neng-Chang Wei,Fei Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了先前对γp → K*Σ反应的研究，将LEPS合作组的自旋密度矩阵元数据纳入分析，以更严格地约束理论模型；结果发现Δ(1905)5/2⁺共振在所有合理拟合中均起关键作用，但κ介子t-道交换的贡献在两种等价拟合中呈现显著分歧，从而质疑了此前认为LEPS的P_σ数据支持κ主导的结论，并给出了8.5 GeV下P_σ的可检验预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过引入LEPS Collaboration提供的γp → K^{*0}Σ^+反应的自旋密度矩阵元（SDME）数据，对先前基于微分截面建立的有效拉格朗日模型施加更强约束，以厘清反应机制，特别是t-道κ交换与s-道Δ(1905)5/2⁺共振的相对重要性。

Method: 采用手征有效拉格朗日量框架，包含s-道N、Δ、Δ(1905)5/2⁺，t-道K、κ、K^*交换，u-道Λ、Σ、Σ^*交换，以及广义接触项；对LEPS SDME数据（E_γ = 1.85–2.96 GeV）进行χ²拟合，获得两组等价最优解并比较其耦合结构。

Result: 得到两个χ²等价的拟合解：二者均要求强Δ(1905)5/2⁺共振贡献；但κ交换振幅在解I中显著，在解II中可忽略；该歧义直接挑战了文献中‘LEPS P_σ数据证实κ主导’的结论；同时预言了E_γ = 8.5 GeV处的P_σ可观测量，可用于未来实验判别κ作用。

Conclusion: Δ(1905)5/2⁺是γp → K^{*0}Σ^+反应中不可替代的动力学成分，而κ交换的角色尚无唯一确定结论；SDME数据虽提升了模型约束力，但仍不足以唯一确定t-道赝标介子交换的贡献，需更高能或更多自旋可观测量（如极化转移系数）进一步甄别。

Abstract: In our earlier work [Phys. Rev. C \textbf{98}, 045209 (2018)], we analyzed the differential cross-section data from the CLAS Collaboration for the reactions $γp \to K^{*+}Σ^0$ and $γp \to K^{*0} Σ^+$ using an effective Lagrangian approach. We found that a satisfactory description of the data required the inclusion of the $s$-channel $Δ(1905)5/2^+$ resonance, in addition to $t$-channel exchanges of $K$, $κ$, and $K^*$, $s$-channel contributions from nucleons ($N$) and $Δ$, $u$-channel exchanges of $Λ$, $Σ$, and $Σ^*$, and a generalized contact term. In the present work, we extend our analysis to incorporate the data on spin density matrix elements from the LEPS Collaboration for the $γp \to K^{*0}Σ^+$ reaction at photon energies $E_γ = 1.85$--$2.96$ GeV. Our goal is to impose more stringent constraints on the theoretical model and obtain a more reliable understanding of the reaction mechanisms. We obtain two fits that describe the experimental data equally well. In both fits, the $Δ(1905)5/2^+$ resonance plays an important role. However, the contribution from $t$-channel $κ$ exchange is significant in one fit but negligible in the other. This finding contradicts earlier claims in the literature that the LEPS parity spin asymmetry $P_σ$ data support a dominant role of $κ$ exchange in $γp \to K^{*0}Σ^+$. We also present predictions for $P_σ$ at $E_γ= 8.5$ GeV, which may help clarify whether $κ$ exchange is indeed dominant in this reaction.

</details>


### [122] [The $B^{(*)}\bar{K}^{(*)}$-coupled-channel system in the hidden-gauge approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28649)
*J. Sánchez-Illana,R. Molina,Pan-Pan Shi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在隐规范形式下预测了底-奇分子态，通过拟合LHCb观测到的一个新激发Bs0态的质量，确定唯一自由参数，并预测了该扇区六个态的极点参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LHCb实验中观测到的新Bs0激发态及约6100和6160 MeV附近的候选态，探索底-奇介子对（B(*), K(*)）形成的强子分子态谱。

Method: 采用隐规范形式（Hidden Gauge Formalism）处理B(*)K(*)耦合道散射，结合唯象动量截断正规化方案，仅引入一个可调参数（标度参数）以拟合已知激发态质量。

Result: 预测了六个分子态的极点位置：BK (0+) 在5760 MeV、B*K (1+) 在5802 MeV；另两个态对应于BK* 和 B*K* 分子结构，其质量与LHCb观测的~6100 MeV 和 ~6160 MeV 结构一致，且质量劈裂符合理论预期。

Conclusion: B(*)K(*)系统存在丰富的近阈分子态谱，D_s0(2317)/D_s1(2460)在底夸克扇区的类比态（即BK/B*K）被明确预言，且LHCb新发现的高质态可自然理解为更高自旋的BK*/B*K*束缚态或共振态。

Abstract: In this work we provide predictions for bottom-strange molecular states within the Hidden Gauge Formalism. We study the coupled-channel scattering of $B^{(*)}\bar{K}^{(*)}$ states and, by fixing only one free parameter to obtain the mass of a new excited $B_s^0$ state seen by the LHCb, we predict the pole parameters of six states in this sector. Concretely, we get that the masses of the flavor partners of the $D_{s0}(2317)$ and $D_{s1}(2460)$ states in the bottom sector are $5760$ and $5802$ MeV for the $B\bar{K}$ ($J^P=0^+$) and $B^{*}\bar{K}$ ($1^+$) states, respectively. Moreover, the recently seen states by the LHCb with masses around $6100$ and $6160$ MeV can be interpreted as $B\bar{K}^*$ and $B^*\bar{K}^*$ molecular states, according to reasonable values of the pole parameters and the splitting between these two states obtained in our calculation.

</details>


### [123] [Hadron spectra and thermodynamics for all quark flavors from a universal Hagedorn temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28668)
*Michał Marczenko,Larry McLerran,Krzysztof Redlich*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了QCD中强子谱由弦动力学决定，其特征是普适的Hagedorn温度与弦张力相关；该机制不仅适用于轻强子和胶球，也统一描述了重味（如粲夸克）强子谱，并在无额外参数下复现格点QCD热力学与实验观测强子谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QCD禁闭动力学在不同夸克质量尺度下的普适性，尤其是重味强子是否服从与轻强子相同的弦激发谱规律。

Method: 通过分离流夸克质量贡献，提取纯禁闭动力学导致的强子能谱，并与格点QCD热力学数据及实验强子质量谱进行对比分析。

Result: 重味强子谱满足同一Hagedorn温度与弦张力关系，分离流夸克质量后，理论谱精确复现粲强子格点热力学与全夸克味强子质量谱。

Conclusion: QCD禁闭动力学具有夸克质量无关的普适性，由弦张力唯一刻画，体现为统一的Hagedorn谱结构。

Abstract: We show that hadrons in QCD follow a spectrum determined by string dynamics characterized by a universal Hagedorn temperature linked to the string tension. While this behavior was recently established for light hadrons and glueballs, we demonstrate that the same dynamics describes the heavy-flavor sector. After separating the current quark masses, the resulting spectrum reproduces lattice QCD thermodynamics of charmed hadrons and the observed spectra of hadrons across quark flavors without additional parameters. These results reflect the universal confining dynamics of QCD through the string tension.

</details>


### [124] [Charge-Dependent Directed Flow in Symmetric Nuclear Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28742)
*Vipul Bairathi,Kishora Nayak*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用改进的AMPT模型研究了多种对称核碰撞中识别强子的定向流v1，重点分析了v1斜率及其电荷依赖劈裂随系统大小和中心度的变化，揭示了高pT区的硬-软不对称性及重子-介子二分现象，并确认v1主要源于部分子相和复合过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相对论重离子碰撞中电荷依赖的定向流劈裂（Δdv1/dy）的物理起源，区分夸克输运效应与电磁场效应的贡献，检验部分子层次动力学在集体流形成中的作用。

Method: 采用弦熔化版本的多相输运模型（AMPT）并改进夸克复合机制，模拟O+O、Cu+Cu、Ru+Ru、Au+Au、U+U在√sNN=200 GeV下的对称核碰撞；提取中快度v1斜率dv1/dy及其电荷劈裂Δdv1/dy，按系统质量数A、中心度及pT区间（低：0.2–2.0 GeV/c；高：2.0–5.0 GeV/c）分类分析；评估末态强子相互作用影响，并与STAR实验Au+Au数据对比。

Result: 低pT区v1斜率弱依赖系统尺寸；高pT区呈现强系统尺寸依赖且随A变负，体现硬-软不对称；Δdv1/dy显示清晰重子-介子二分：p-p̄与Λ-Λ̄劈裂显著且随A增大，π⁺-π⁻与K⁺-K⁻劈裂可忽略；末态强子散射对v1斜率影响可忽略，证实其起源于部分子相与夸克复合；AMPT结果为STAR观测到的电荷依赖v1劈裂提供了夸克输运基线。

Conclusion: 电荷依赖v1劈裂主要由参与复合的价夸克输运决定，重子因含三个价夸克而对初态电荷不对称更敏感，介子则因夸克-反夸克对湮灭/重组而抑制劈裂；该效应构成电磁场信号提取的必要基线，凸显QGP早期部分子动力学的关键作用。

Abstract: The directed flow ($v_1$) of identified hadrons ($π^{\pm}, K^{\pm}, p, \bar{p}, φ, Λ$, and $\barΛ$) is studied in symmetric nuclear collisions (O+O, Cu+Cu, Ru+Ru, Au+Au, and U+U) at $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 200$ GeV using the string-melting version of a multiphase transport model with improved quark coalescence. The mid-rapidity $v_1$-slope ($dv_1/dy$) and its charge-dependent splitting ($Δdv_1/dy$) between particles and anti-particles are investigated as a function of nuclear mass number ($A$) and collision centrality in both low-$p_\mathrm{T}$ (0.2$-$2.0 GeV/$c$) and high-$p_\mathrm{T}$ (2.0$-$5.0 GeV/$c$) regions. At low-$p_\mathrm{T}$, the $v_1$-slope shows weak system-size dependence, while at high-$p_\mathrm{T}$ strong system-size dependence is found and it becomes negative with nuclear mass number, reflecting the hard-soft asymmetry in particle production. The charge-dependent splitting $Δdv_1/dy$ reveals a striking baryon-meson dichotomy: baryon pairs ($p-\bar{p}$ and $Λ-\barΛ$) exhibit significant splitting that grows with system size, whereas meson pairs ($π^+-π^-$ and $K^+-K^-$) show minimal splitting. The effect of final state hadronic interactions on the $v_1$-slope is found to be negligible confirming that it is primarily generated during the partonic phase and coalescence process. A comparison of the AMPT results with measurements from the STAR experiment at RHIC in Au+Au collisions establish the transported quark contribution as a baseline for the observed charge-dependent $v_1$ splitting, on top of which electromagnetic field effects must be considered.

</details>
